Let's design `piecewise_search(m, n)`:
- Variables: block boundaries t_j (cumulative fraction) and heights h_j. To avoid overparameter, use uniform boundaries initially but optimize heights and maybe lengths via softmax of boundary variables.
- Simpler: fix n and represent sequence as blocks of equal width L=n/m. Optimize heights h_j (m variables) using DE/projected gradient. This is a lower-dimensional problem that may find good block patterns. Then exact score on full n (with block expansion). Since block width L maybe >1; if score of block pattern (not expanded) maybe? We can just evaluate expanded sequence exact. We can also allow m=n (full) in later refine.
- Equal-width blocks reduce sequence smoothness; uniform width may be okay. The optimum might have variable widths, but equal blocks with many m can approximate.
- Use m=16 or32, n=256 (L=8) or n=1024 (L=16). If found expanded score <1.5, good.

import numpy as np
import time
try: from scipy.optimize import linprog
except: linprog=None

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    deadline = time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s-2)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best = default_sequence() # two-block 256
    best_score = fast_score(best)
    # block searches
    for n,m in [(128,16),(256,32),(512,64)]:
       patterns = initial_patterns(m)
       run DE for time slice depending on budget and if best_score >1.5 etc.
       if found score < best_score: update best, best_score
    # full local search on some copies of best (downscale to 128/256 if best large)
    for arr in candidates:
       refine with projected gradient + random blocks for time slice
    # LP refine for best if n<=128
    # try expanding best to larger lengths and exact eval
    # final exact verification, if score inf? adjust.
    return [float(x) for x in best]
```

Detailed helpers:

`fast_conv(a)`:

n=a.size
N=1
while N < 2*n-1: N <<= 1
fa=np.fft.rfftnormalized? no, np.fft.rfft(a, N)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2*n-1]
# due numerical, set negatives small to 0? max positive.
return c
```
`fast_score(a)`:
```
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-12: return inf
c=fast_conv(a)
m=c.max()
return 2.0*n*m/(s*s)
```

`project_simplex(v)`: standard.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    x=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=x.size
    u=np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)+1) > (cv+1))[0][-1]
    theta=(cv[rho]+1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(x-theta,0)
```
This projects onto sum1. If x all? works. If no? Return uniform.

`derivative_from_weights(a, weights_lags)`:
```
n=a.size
grad=np.zeros(n)
for k,w in active:
   if w<=0: continue
   lo=max(0,k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
   for i in range(lo,hi+1):
       grad[i] += w*a[k-i]
# derivative of c sum; if using logsumexp, factor beta*2; we multiply later.
grad *= 2.0
return grad
```
For n=256, topK=5, loops 1280 per iter. Fine.

`smooth_objective` not needed.

Projected gradient refine:
```
def projected_gradient_refine(a, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
   n=a.size; best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(best)
   T=(best_score - 0.5)*0.2
   beta=10
   eta_options=[0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]
   it=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      # update beta every 1000
      it+=1
      if it%500==0: beta=min(beta*1.2,200); T*=0.9
      c=fast_conv(best)
      maxidx=np.argpartition(c, -5)[-5:]
      top_vals=c[maxidx]
      # weights softmax beta*(top-max)
      raw=np.exp(beta*(top_vals-top_vals.max()))
      w=raw/raw.sum()
      grad=derivative_from_weights(best, zip(maxidx,w))
      gm=grad - grad.mean()
      accepted=False
      for eta in eta_options:
         cand=np.clip(best - eta*gm, 0.0, None)
         s=cand.sum()
         if s<=0: continue
         cand=project_simplex(cand) # ensures sum1
         sc=fast_score(cand)
         if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < best_score or np.random.random() < np.exp(-max(0,sc-best_score)/max(T,1e-6))):
             best=cand; best_score=sc; accepted=True; break
      if not accepted:
         # random block perturbation
         B=rng.integers(4, max(5,n//4))
         start=rng.integers(0,n-B)
         cand=best.copy()
         # multiply segment by exp normal or set random convex mix
         if rng.random()<0.5:
             mult=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,size=B))
             cand[start:start+B]*=mult
         else:
             # move mass
             delta=(cand[start:start+B]*0.5)
             # add to another segment
             start2=rng.integers(0,n-B)
             cand[start:start+B] -= delta
             cand[start2:start2+B] += delta
         cand=np.clip(cand,0,None)
         s=cand.sum(); if s>0: cand=cand/s
         sc=fast_score(cand)
         if sc<best_score or anneal: accept
      if it%2000==0: print
   return best,best_score
```
Potential: `fast_score` normalizes cand; if cand zero invalid.
Anneal acceptance uses T. Need avoid epsilon.

def de_search(obj_fn, dim, lower, upper, rng, time_limit, start_vectors, pop_factor=8, F=0.8, CR=0.7):
    pop = max(20, pop_factor*dim)
    X=[]; F_=[]

          # crossover

          for j in range(dim):

Random block perturbation details:
- Segment length proportional n: `width = int(rng.integers(max(2,n//64), max(3,n//8)))`.
- Type1 multiply segment by exp(N(0,sigma)), sigma 0.1-0.3.
- Type2 linearly interpolate between current and random positive values: segment = (1-α)a + α*rand.
- Type3 move mass: reduce segment by factor, add another.

g=res.x

`derive_grad`:
```
def conv_gradient_from_lags(a, lags, weights):
    n=a.size
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for k,w in zip(lags,weights):
        if w<=0: continue
        lo=max(0, k-(n-1)); hi=min(n-1,k)
        # i from lo to hi
        # grad[lo:hi+1] += w * a[k-lo : k-hi? reversed]
        # Since j=k-i ranges k-lo down to k-hi.
        seg = a[k-lo:k-hi+1][::-1]
        grad[lo:hi+1] += w * seg
    grad *= 2.0
    return grad
```
Check: For k fixed, i=lo..hi, j=k-i. a[j] sequence for i increasing: j decreasing. `a[k-lo:k-hi+1]` length hi-lo+1. k-lo = hi? Example n5,k3, lo0,hi4, slice a[3:-1+1]? a[3:4] only length1 wrong. Let's manually compute. Need j from k-lo down to k-hi inclusive. The slice `a[k-hi : k-lo+1]` ascending, then reverse. Example k3, lo0,hi4: k-hi=-1, k-lo+1=4 -> a[-1:4] gives none? Python weird. Better loop small, n up to512 direct loops okay. But for speed, handle properly:
```
js = k - np.arange(lo, hi+1)
seg = a[js]
grad[lo:hi+1] += w*seg
```
Allocating js each lag; fine.
```

```
def add_candidate(arr, score, cand_list, max_items=12):
  arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
  if arr.size==0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return
  sumv=arr.sum()
  if sumv<=0: return
  arr=arr/sumv # store normalized sum1
  if score not finite: score=fast_score(arr)
  item=(float(score), arr.copy())
  if len(cand_list) < max_items: cand_list.append(item); return
  worst=0; worst_score=cand_list[0][0]
  for idx in range(1,len(cand_list)):
     if cand_list[idx][0] > worst_score: worst=idx; worst_score=cand_list[idx]
  cand_list[worst]=item
```

Let's write DE search carefully without nested functions.

```
def de_search_with_args(obj_fn, args, bounds, rng, time_limit, start_vectors, verbose=False):
    if time_limit <=0: return None, inf, 0
    dim=bounds[0]
    lower=float(bounds[1]); upper=float(bounds[2])
    lo=np.full(dim, lower); up=np.full(dim, upper)
    pop_size=max(24, 8*dim)
    X=[]; vals=[]
    # add starts
    for v in start_vectors:
       if len(X) >= pop_size: break
       x=np.asarray(v, float)
       if x.size != dim: continue
       x=np.clip(x, lo, up)
       x=x - np.mean(x) # canonical? But bounds? If mean shift could exceed; clip again.
       x=np.clip(x, lo, up)
       try: val=obj_fn(x,*args)
       except Exception: val=inf
       X.append(x.copy()); vals.append(val)
    while len(X)<pop_size:
       x=rng.uniform(lower, upper, size=dim)
       x=x - np.mean(x); x=np.clip(x,lo,up)
       val=...
    best_i=0; for i...
    best_x=X[0].copy(); best_f=vals[0]
    end=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    gen=0; evals=0
    while time.monotonic() < end:
       F=0.9 - 0.35*min(1.0, gen/100.0)
       CR=0.7
       for i in range(pop_size):
          if time.monotonic() >= end: break
          # pick r1,r2
          r1=rng.integers(0,pop_size)
          while r1==i: r1=...
          r2=rng.integers(0,pop_size)
          while r2==i or r2==r1: ...
          mutant = X[i] + F*(X[r1]-X[r2])
          trial = X[i].copy()
          j0=int(rng.integers(dim))
          for j in range(dim):
             if rng.random() < CR or j == j0:
                trial[j]=mutant[j]
          trial=np.clip(trial,lo,up)
          trial=trial - np.mean(trial)
          trial=np.clip(trial,lo,up)
          try: val=obj_fn(trial,*args)
          except: val=inf
          evals+=1
          if val < vals[i]:
             X[i]=trial; vals[i]=val
             if val < best_f:
                best_f=val; best_x=trial.copy(); best_i=i
       gen +=1
       if verbose and gen%25==0: print
    return best_x,best_f,evals
```
Canonical mean centering may make values exceed bounds; clip. But if centering, the shape from softmax invariant to constants; okay. If all starts mean-centered, good.

Potential performance: For each trial we do mean and clip; okay.

mask = rng.random(dim) < CR; mask[j0]=True
trial = np.where(mask, mutant, X[i])
```

def obj_block(z, n, m):
    L = n // m
    if L <=0: return inf
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if z.size != m: return inf
    z=np.clip(z, -30, 30)
    z = z - np.mean(z)
    h=np.exp(z)
    s=h.sum()
    if s <=1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s): return inf
    h=h/s
    # maybe enforce min? If h has zeros due underflow, exp after mean center maybe all positive unless z very negative; okay.
    a=np.repeat(h, L)
    # if a.size != n (if n not multiple) return inf? We choose n multiples.
    return fast_score(a)
```
Since h normalized sum1, a sum L, but if h all equal 1/m, a uniform. Good.

`lp_maxsum`:
```
def lp_maxsum(a, M_target):
   if linprog is None: return None
   n=a.size
   if M_target <=0: return np.zeros(n)
   G=np.zeros((2*n-1,n), dtype=float)
   for i in range(n):
      ai=a[i]
      if ai != 0.0:
         G[i:i+n, i] = ai
   # If some rows all zero? fine.
   c_obj = -np.ones(n)
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=G, b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, M_target), bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
   except Exception:
      return None
   if not hasattr(res,'x') or res.status not in (0,3)? HiGHS status 0 optimal. If 3? skip.
   g=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   g=np.where(g>0, g, 0.0)
   return g
```
The bounds list creation `[(0.0,None)]*n` okay (tuples immutable). If n=128 fine.

Potential memory for `bounds` as list large but okay. Could pass `bounds=(0.0,None)` list of n? It expects sequence of tuples. Good.

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step-function coefficients.

The objective is
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2
where conv is numpy's full linear convolution.

This program uses several complementary numerical methods:
  1. Block-wise global search (differential evolution) on coarse piecewise-
     constant densities, expanded to a fixed fine length.
  2. Full-length projected gradient / smooth log-sum-exp local search with
     simulated annealing and random block perturbations.
  3. A convex linear-programming projection step inspired by the previous
     state-of-the-art construction.
  4. Final exact scoring and a few exact expansion/downsampling checks.

The returned sequence is normalized to sum 1, but the evaluator is scale
invariant, so any positive scale would also work.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None


# -------------------------
# Fast score / convolution
# -------------------------

def next_power2(n):
    p = 1
    while p < n:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fast_conv(a):
    """Fast full linear convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power2(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:need]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c


def fast_score(a):
    """Scale-invariant score using FFT. Sequence is normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    c = fast_conv(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def exact_score(a):
    """Use the authoritative evaluator if available."""
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
        val = float(val)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return val
    except Exception:
        pass
    return fast_score(a)


# ---------------------------
# Basic sequence constructors
# ---------------------------

def two_block_sequence(n):
    a = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return a
    k = max(1, a.size // 2)
    a[:k] = 2.0
    s = a.sum()
    if s > 0:
        a /= s
    return a


def smooth_start(n, cycles=3):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    a = 1.0 + 0.9 * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    a = a * (1.0 + 0.4 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(cycles) * x))
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-5, None)
    s = a.sum()
    if s > 0:
        a /= s
    return a


def random_nonnegative(n, rng):
    a = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=int(n))) + 1e-6
    s = a.sum()
    if s > 0:
        a /= s
    return a


def downsample(a, target):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    s = a.sum()
    if s > 0:
        a = a / s
    n = a.size
    if n <= int(target) or n == 0:
        return a
    stride = max(1, n // int(target))
    take = int(target) * stride
    if take > n:
        take = n
    if take == 0:
        return a
    out = a[:take].reshape(int(target), stride).mean(axis=1)
    ss = out.sum()
    if ss > 0:
        out /= ss
    return out


# ------------------------------
# Simplex and gradient helpers
# ------------------------------

def project_simplex(v):
    """Project a vector onto the probability simplex in 1-D."""
    x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = x.size
    if n == 0:
        return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    u = np.sort(x)[::-1]
    cv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.nonzero((1.0 + np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)) * u > (cv + 1.0))[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(idx[-1])
    theta = (cv[rho] + 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(x - theta, 0.0)
    ss = out.sum()
    if ss <= 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / ss


def conv_gradient_from_lags(a, lags, weights):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k weights[k] * (a*a)[k] under fixed support length.
    For c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}, d c_k / d a_i = 2 a_{k-i} when valid.
    """
    n = a.size
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, w in zip(lags, weights):
        if w <= 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        if k < 0 or k >= 2 * n - 1:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        hi = min(n - 1, k)
        if hi < lo:
            continue
        idx = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        grad[lo:hi + 1] += w * a[k - idx]
    grad *= 2.0
    return grad


def random_block_candidate(a, rng):
    """Random positive perturbation block move for escaping local minima."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 2:
        return np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.1, size=n))
    bmin = 2
    bmax = max(bmin + 1, min(n - 1, max(3, n // 16)))
    B = int(rng.integers(bmin, bmax + 1))
    B = min(B, max(2, n - 1))
    s = int(rng.integers(0, n - B + 1))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
    cand = a.copy()

    if mode == 0:
        mult = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.18, size=B))
        cand[s:s + B] *= mult
    elif mode == 1:
        move = 0.25 * a[s:s + B]
        cand[s:s + B] -= move
        s2 = int(rng.integers(0, n - B + 1))
        cand[s2:s2 + B] += move
    else:
        mean = float(np.mean(a[s:s + B])) + 1e-8
        newv = np.abs(rng.normal(mean, max(1e-8, 0.1 * mean), size=B))
        cand[s:s + B] = 0.5 * a[s:s + B] + 0.5 * newv

    cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
    ss = cand.sum()
    if ss <= 0:
        cand = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        ss = 1.0
    return cand / ss


# -----------------------------------
# Full-length local refinement
# -----------------------------------

def full_local_search(a, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    """Projective gradient + annealing local search on the full vector."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, fast_score(a)

    s = a.sum()
    cur = a / s if s > 0 else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    cur = project_simplex(cur)
    cur_score = fast_score(cur)
    best = cur.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    T = max(1e-4, (best_score - 0.5) * 0.12)
    base_eta = 0.18
    beta = 24.0
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        if it % 800 == 0:
            beta = min(beta * 1.22, 350.0)
            T *= 0.92

        c = fast_conv(cur)
        topk = min(12, c.size)
        top_idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
        top_c = c[top_idx]
        mxc = float(np.max(top_c))
        ex = np.exp(beta * (top_c - mxc))
        zsum = float(np.sum(ex))
        if not np.isfinite(zsum) or zsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(topk, 1.0 / topk, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = ex / zsum

        grad = conv_gradient_from_lags(cur, top_idx, w)
        gm = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gm))

        accepted_any = False
        if gnorm > 1e-15:
            for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.2, 0.08):
                step = base_eta * mult
                cand = cur - step * gm
                cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
                ss = cand.sum()
                if ss <= 0:
                    continue
                cand = project_simplex(cand)
                sc = fast_score(cand)
                if (sc < cur_score - 1e-14) or (
                    np.isfinite(sc) and float(rng.random()) < float(np.exp(-max(0.0, sc - cur_score) / max(T, 1e-8)))
                ):
                    cur = cand
                    cur_score = sc
                    accepted_any = True
                    if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        best = cand.copy()
                        best_score = sc
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

        if not accepted_any:
            no_improve += 1
            base_eta *= 0.78
            if base_eta < 0.002:
                base_eta = 0.02

        if no_improve > 35:
            cand = random_block_candidate(cur, rng)
            sc = fast_score(cand)
            if sc < cur_score * 0.98 or (
                np.isfinite(sc) and float(rng.random()) < float(np.exp(-max(0.0, sc - cur_score) / max(T, 1e-8)))
            ):
                cur = cand
                cur_score = sc
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = cur.copy()
                best_score = sc
            no_improve = 0
            base_eta = max(0.03, base_eta * 1.5)

        if cur_score > best_score + 0.35:
            cur = best.copy()
            cur_score = best_score
            base_eta = max(0.03, base_eta * 0.5)

        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            print(
                "full_local n={} it={} best=%.10f cur=%.10f"
                % (n, it, best_score, cur_score),
                flush=True
            )

    return best, best_score


# -------------------------------
# Linear-programming projection
# -------------------------------

def lp_maxsum(a, M_target):
    """
    Solve: max sum(g) s.t. a*g <= M_target, g >= 0.
    Returns g array or None.
    """
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    if M_target <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M_target):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    G = np.zeros((2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(n):
        ai = a[i]
        if ai != 0.0:
            G[i:i + n, i] = ai

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(M_target), dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=G,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if not hasattr(res, "x") or res.status != 0:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g) & (g > 0.0), g, 0.0)
    return g


def lp_refine(a, time_limit, verbose=False):
    """
    Convex LP projection step, following the previous state-of-the-art idea:
    find g maximizing sum(g) under old cross-convolution bound a*g <= M,
    then mix.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = a.sum()
    if n == 0 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return a, fast_score(a)
    if linprog is None or n > 192:
        return a, fast_score(a)
    if s <= 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = fast_score(a)
    cur = a.copy()
    cur_score = best_score

    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    it = 0
    max_iters = 80 + 2 * n

    while time.monotonic() < end and it < max_iters:
        c = fast_conv(cur)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        progressed = False

        # Use current M first, as in the SOTA formulation.
        g = lp_maxsum(cur, M * (1.0 + 1e-7))
        if g is not None:
            gs = float(g.sum())
            if gs > 1e-12:
                gN = g / gs
                mix_scores = []
                for t in (0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5):
                    cand = (1.0 - t) * cur + t * gN
                    cand = project_simplex(cand)
                    sc = fast_score(cand)
                    mix_scores.append((sc, cand))
                mix_scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                for sc, cand in mix_scores:
                    if sc < cur_score - 1e-14:
                        cur = cand
                        cur_score = sc
                        progressed = True
                        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best = cand.copy()
                            best_score = sc
                        break

        # If current-M mixing did not improve, try slightly tightened bounds.
        if not progressed:
            for factor in (0.995, 0.99, 0.98, 0.97, 0.95):
                Mt = M * factor
                g = lp_maxsum(cur, Mt)
                if g is None:
                    continue
                gs = float(g.sum())
                if gs < 1.0 - 1e-7:
                    continue
                gN = g / gs
                mix_scores = []
                for t in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35):
                    cand = (1.0 - t) * cur + t * gN
                    cand = project_simplex(cand)
                    sc = fast_score(cand)
                    mix_scores.append((sc, cand))
                if not mix_scores:
                    continue
                mix_scores.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
                sc, cand = mix_scores[0]
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = cand.copy()
                    best_score = sc
                    cur = cand.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
                    progressed = True
                    break

        it += 1
        if verbose and it % 5 == 0:
            print("lp_refine it=%d M=%.12f best=%.10f" % (it, M, best_score), flush=True)

        if not progressed:
            break

    return best, best_score


# ---------------------------
# Block-wise global search
# ---------------------------

def pattern_to_z(pattern):
    h = np.asarray(pattern, dtype=np.float64)
    if h.size == 0:
        return h
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    h = np.clip(h, 1e-12, 1e6)
    s = h.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return np.zeros_like(h, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.log(h / s)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    return z


def initial_block_patterns(m, rng):
    out = []
    n = int(m)

    out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    h = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = max(1, n // 2)
    h[:k] = 2.0
    out.append(h)

    h = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = max(1, n // 4)
    h[:k] = 3.0
    out.append(h)

    h = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    k1 = max(1, n // 6)
    k2 = max(k1 + 1, n // 3)
    h[:k1] = 3.0
    h[k1:k2] = 2.0
    out.append(h)

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    out.append(np.clip(1.0 + 0.95 * np.cos(np.pi * x), 0.01, None))
    out.append(np.clip(1.0 + 0.95 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.01, None))
    out.append(np.clip(1.0 + 0.95 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x), 0.01, None))
    out.append(np.clip(1.0 + 0.95 * np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x), 0.01, None))

    for _ in range(4):
        h = np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)) + 0.05
        out.append(h)

    return out


def expand_z(z, n, m):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(m)
    n = int(n)
    L = n // m
    if L <= 0 or z.size != m:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=20.0, neginf=-20.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    h = np.exp(z)
    s = h.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        h = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / m
    else:
        h = h / s
    a = np.repeat(h, L)
    ss = a.sum()
    if ss > 0:
        a /= ss
    return a


def obj_block(z, n, m):
    n = int(n)
    m = int(m)
    if z.size != m or n <= 0 or m <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = expand_z(z, n, m)
    return fast_score(a)


def de_search_with_args(obj_fn, args, bounds, rng, time_limit, start_vectors, verbose=False):
    """
    Custom differential evolution with extra args.
    bounds = (dim, lower, upper)
    """
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf"), 0

    dim = int(bounds[0])
    lower = float(bounds[1])
    upper = float(bounds[2])
    lo = np.full(dim, lower, dtype=np.float64)
    up = np.full(dim, upper, dtype=np.float64)

    pop_size = max(24, 8 * dim)
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    X = []
    vals = []

    def _eval_vec(v):
        try:
            val = obj_fn(v, *args)
            val = float(val)
            if not np.isfinite(val):
                return 1e300
            return val
        except Exception:
            return 1e300

    # Here an inner local function is used for clarity; it is not exposed as a
    # top-level helper and does not capture mutable pending-closure data beyond
    # the passed args. This is safe in Python, but if desired it can be inlined.
    # (No lambda is used.)

    if start_vectors:
        for v in start_vectors:
            if len(X) >= pop_size:
                break
            x = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
            if x.size != dim:
                continue
            x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=upper, neginf=lower)
            x = np.clip(x, lo, up)
            x = x - float(np.mean(x))
            x = np.clip(x, lo, up)
            X.append(x.copy())
            vals.append(_eval_vec(x))

    while len(X) < pop_size:
        x = rng.uniform(lower, upper, size=dim)
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        x = np.clip(x, lo, up)
        X.append(x.copy())
        vals.append(_eval_vec(x))

    best_i = 0
    for i in range(pop_size):
        if vals[i] < vals[best_i]:
            best_i = i
    best_f = vals[best_i]
    best_x = X[best_i].copy()

    gen = 0
    evals = len(X)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        F = 0.9 - 0.35 * min(1.0, float(gen) / 80.0)
        CR = 0.7

        for i in range(pop_size):
            if time.monotonic() >= end:
                break

            r1 = int(rng.integers(0, pop_size))
            while r1 == i:
                r1 = int(rng.integers(0, pop_size))
            r2 = int(rng.integers(0, pop_size))
            while r2 == i or r2 == r1:
                r2 = int(rng.integers(0, pop_size))

            mutant = X[i] + F * (X[r1] - X[r2])
            mask = rng.random(dim) < CR
            j0 = int(rng.integers(dim))
            mask[j0] = True
            trial = np.where(mask, mutant, X[i])
            trial = np.nan_to_num(trial, nan=0.0, posinf=upper, neginf=lower)
            trial = np.clip(trial, lo, up)
            trial = trial - float(np.mean(trial))
            trial = np.clip(trial, lo, up)

            val = _eval_vec(trial)
            evals += 1
            if val < vals[i] - 1e-15:
                X[i] = trial.copy()
                vals[i] = val
                if val < best_f - 1e-15:
                    best_f = val
                    best_x = trial.copy()
                    best_i = i

        gen += 1
        if verbose and gen % 25 == 0:
            print("DE gen=%d evals=%d best=%.10f" % (gen, evals, best_f), flush=True)
            if time.monotonic() >= end:
                break

    return best_x, best_f, evals


# -----------------------------
# Candidate bookkeeping
# -----------------------------

def add_candidate(arr, score, cand_list, max_items=16):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = arr.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        return
    arr = arr / s
    if not np.isfinite(float(score)):
        score = fast_score(arr)
    item = (float(score), arr.copy())

    if len(cand_list) < max_items:
        cand_list.append(item)
        return

    worst_idx = 0
    worst_score = cand_list[0][0]
    for i in range(1, len(cand_list)):
        if cand_list[i][0] > worst_score:
            worst_idx = i
            worst_score = cand_list[i][0]
    cand_list[worst_idx] = item


def main_round_of_search(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    budget = max(15.0, float(budget_s) - 3.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + budget

    candidates = []

    # Robust fallback / warm start.
    fallback = two_block_sequence(256)
    fallback_score = fast_score(fallback)
    if np.isfinite(fallback_score):
        add_candidate(fallback, fallback_score, candidates, 16)
        best_fast = fallback.copy()
        best_score = fallback_score
    else:
        best_fast = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0
        best_score = fast_score(best_fast)
        add_candidate(best_fast, best_score, candidates, 16)

    print("initial fallback score = %.10f" % best_score, flush=True)

    # If a global parent exists, downsample it as an extra start.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim != 1 or parent.size == 0:
            parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ps = parent.sum()
        if ps > 0:
            psc = downsample(parent, 128)
            psc_score = fast_score(psc)
            if np.isfinite(psc_score) and psc_score < best_score:
                best_fast = psc
                best_score = psc_score
            add_candidate(psc, psc_score, candidates, 16)

    # --------------------
    # Block global searches
    # --------------------
    block_configs = [
        # m, fine_n, budget_fraction
        (8, 64, 0.018),
        (16, 128, 0.050),
        (32, 256, 0.165),
        (64, 512, 0.300),
    ]

    for m, n, frac in block_configs:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 5.0:
            break
        limit = min(budget * frac, rem - 40.0 if rem > 50.0 else rem * 0.35)
        if limit <= 2.0:
            continue

        patterns = initial_block_patterns(m, rng)
        z_starts = []
        for p in patterns:
            z_starts.append(pattern_to_z(p))
        # Include a downsample of the current best if compatible.
        try:
            dd = downsample(best_fast, m)
            if dd.size == m and np.isfinite(dd).all() and dd.sum() > 0:
                z_starts.append(pattern_to_z(dd))
        except Exception:
            pass

        best_z, best_f, _ = de_search_with_args(
            obj_block,
            (n, m),
            (m, -30.0, 30.0),
            rng,
            limit,
            z_starts,
            verbose=False,
        )

        if best_z is not None and np.isfinite(best_f):
            a = expand_z(best_z, n, m)
            sc = fast_score(a)
            print("block m=%d n=%d score=%.10f" % (m, n, sc), flush=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate(a, sc, candidates, 16)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_fast = a
                    best_score = sc

    # --------------------
    # Full-length local refine
    # --------------------
    ref_start_a = two_block_sequence(128)
    ref_start_score = fast_score(ref_start_a)
    add_candidate(ref_start_a, ref_start_score, candidates, 16)

    ref_start_b = smooth_start(128, 3)
    ref_start_score = fast_score(ref_start_b)
    add_candidate(ref_start_b, ref_start_score, candidates, 16)

    ref_start_c = smooth_start(256, 5)
    ref_start_score = fast_score(ref_start_c)
    add_candidate(ref_start_c, ref_start_score, candidates, 16)

    # Add current best if its length is reasonable for refine.
    if best_fast.size >= 32 and best_fast.size <= 1024:
        pass
    ref_starts = [ref_start_a.copy(), ref_start_b.copy(), ref_start_c.copy()]
    if best_fast.size >= 32 and best_fast.size <= 1024:
        ref_starts.append(best_fast.copy())

    ref_budgets = [0.08, 0.06, 0.10, 0.10]
    total_ref = sum(ref_budgets)
    for i, arr in enumerate(ref_starts):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 5.0:
            break
        limit = min(budget * (ref_budgets[i] / total_ref), rem - 60.0 if rem > 70.0 else rem * 0.5)
        if limit <= 2.0:
            continue
        out, sc = full_local_search(arr, limit, rng, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            add_candidate(out, sc, candidates, 16)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_fast = out
                best_score = sc
        print("full refine n=%s score=%.10f" % (arr.size, sc), flush=True)

    # --------------------
    # LP projection refine
    # --------------------
    lp_starts = []
    lp_starts.append(two_block_sequence(128))
    lp_starts.append(smooth_start(128, 3))
    if best_fast.size >= 64:
        lp_starts.append(downsample(best_fast, 128))
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 64:
        lp_starts.append(downsample(parent, 128))

    # De-duplicate by simple average to avoid trivial repeats.
    cleaned = []
    for x in lp_starts:
        y = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        if y.size != 128 or not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            continue
        s = y.sum()
        if s <= 0:
            continue
        y = y / s
        dup = False
        for z in cleaned:
            if np.allclose(y, z, atol=1e-8):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            cleaned.append(y)

    lp_budget_each = budget * 0.055
    for x in cleaned[:4]:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 5.0:
            break
        limit = min(lp_budget_each, rem - 30.0 if rem > 40.0 else rem * 0.5)
        if limit <= 1.0:
            continue
        out, sc = lp_refine(x, limit, verbose=True)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            add_candidate(out, sc, candidates, 16)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_fast = out
                best_score = sc
        print("LP refine score=%.10f" % sc, flush=True)

    # --------------------
    # Use remaining time for best refinement
    # --------------------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 45.0 and best_score > 1.50:
        extra = min(700.0, rem - 20.0)
        if extra > 20.0:
            out, sc = full_local_search(best_fast.copy(), extra, rng, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate(out, sc, candidates, 16)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_fast = out
                    best_score = sc
            print("extra full refine score=%.10f" % sc, flush=True)

    # --------------------
    # Try simple expansions / downsamplings
    # --------------------
    def consider_candidate_by_fast(arr):
        nonlocal best_fast, best_score
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0:
            return
        s = arr.sum()
        if s <= 0:
            return
        arr = arr / s
        sc = fast_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            add_candidate(arr, sc, candidates, 16)
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_fast = arr
                best_score = sc
        return sc

    # local helper? To avoid nested function in top-level style, do manually.
    def _no_op():
        pass

    # Expansions of the current best.
    if best_fast.size <= 2048:
        for factor in (2, 4):
            if best_fast.size * factor <= 4096:
                cand = np.repeat(best_fast, factor)
                s = cand.sum()
                if s > 0:
                    cand /= s
                    sc = fast_score(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        add_candidate(cand, sc, candidates, 16)
                        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_fast = cand
                            best_score = sc

    if best_fast.size % 2 == 0 and best_fast.size >= 64:
        cand = downsample(best_fast, best_fast.size // 2)
        s = cand.sum()
        if s > 0:
            cand /= s
            sc = fast_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                add_candidate(cand, sc, candidates, 16)
                if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_fast = cand
                    best_score = sc

    # Exact final selection.
    best_exact = float("inf")
    best_arr = best_fast

    for sc, arr in candidates:
        arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size == 0 or arr.size > 8000:
            continue
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = arr.sum()
        if s <= 0:
            continue
        arr = arr / s
        es = exact_score(arr)
        if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
            best_exact = es
            best_arr = arr.copy()

    # Include the tracked best as well, in case it was omitted.
    es = exact_score(best_fast)
    if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact:
        best_exact = es
        best_arr = best_fast.copy()

    # If exact evaluator was unavailable or failed, fall back to fast best.
    if best_arr is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_arr)):
        best_arr = best_fast

    # Final sanity normalization.
    best_arr = np.asarray(best_arr, dtype=np.float64)
    best_arr = np.clip(best_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = best_arr.sum()
    if ss <= 0:
        best_arr = two_block_sequence(256)
    else:
        best_arr = best_arr / ss

    print("final tracked fast score=%.10f" % best_score, flush=True)
    try:
        print("final exact score=%.10f" % float(exact_score(best_arr)), flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in best_arr]


# -------------------------
# Required entry point
# -------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for the best sequence under the supplied time budget.
    """
    try:
        return main_round_of_search(seed=seed, budget_s=float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        # Absolute fallback: fixed two-block sequence with score 16/9.
        a = two_block_sequence(256)
        return [float(x) for x in a]
```
